**b32 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — App payment करने का software interface है; bank transfer खाते में रकम पहुँचाता है। Currency पैसा है, जबकि यह voucher तय सेवा लेने का हक है। इसलिए phone पर दिखने वाली इन चारों चीजों को एक category में मत रखिए।
  _screen: App: interface | Transfer: movement into an account | Currency: money | e-RUPI voucher: specified service entitlement._

**b33 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — Coupon की तुलना में 2 शर्तें जोड़िए: तय beneficiary ही इस्तेमाल करे, वह भी तय सेवा के लिए।
  _screen: Gift-coupon analogy + intended beneficiary only + intended service only._

**b34 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — खास trap है e-RUPI और Digital Rupee: मिलते-जुलते नाम से voucher currency नहीं बनता।
  _screen: e-RUPI voucher ≠ Digital Rupee._

**b35 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Table में app interface है, transfer रकम पहुँचाता है, currency पैसा है; e-RUPI तय सेवा का voucher है। Gift coupon समझिए, लेकिन beneficiary और purpose के दोनों ताले रखिए।
  _screen: Difference table: Instrument | Function | e-RUPI distinction. Payment app | Software interface | Voucher is not the app. Bank transfer | Moves funds into an account | Voucher substitutes a restricted entitlement. Digital currency | Money in digital form | Voucher is not currency. e-RUPI voucher | Redeem for specified service | Intended beneficiary + intended purpose. Gift-coupon analogy retains both locks._

### Part c31.3 · process
_frame: process: Trace the vaccination voucher from government support to redemption. · how: Government sends a ₹1,000 SMS or QR voucher; the recipient redeems it at an authorised centre. · when: Allocation and delivery precede redemption. · conditions: Cash withdrawal and unrelated purchases are excluded. · atmosphere: A hypothetical COVID vaccination transaction, not a reported programme._

**b36 · question_to_student · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — अब delivery और result अलग देखिए: message पहुँचना vaccination मिलना नहीं है।
  _screen: Separate delivery milestone from service received._

**b37 · explain · step 2: inputs and the place** — सरकार fund करती है, beneficiary voucher लाता है, authorised centre सेवा देता है; ₹1,000 hypothetical रकम है, universal limit नहीं।
  _screen: Actors and inputs: government funding; beneficiary's voucher; authorised service centre. Illustrative ₹1,000._

**b38 · process_walkthrough · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — Funding पहले चाहिए ताकि voucher prepaid हो; delivery के बाद redemption में उस हक के बदले सेवा मिलती है।
  _screen: Explain causal links on existing example: funding enables prepaid entitlement; redemption obtains service._

**b39 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Cash माँगने पर काम क्यों रुकेगा? वह तय सेवा के बदले redemption नहीं है।
  _screen: Attempt cash withdrawal: purpose condition fails._

**b40 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — एक बार तेजी से: सरकार ने voucher भेजा, आपने centre पर दिखाया, vaccination मिली।
  _screen: Single fast replay of the existing vaccination transaction._

**b41 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — Flow की आखिरी कड़ी redemption है, सिर्फ phone पर delivery नहीं।
  _screen: Flow recap: funded entitlement → delivery → authorised service redemption. Highlight final link._

### Part c31.4 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Identify permitted and excluded uses of the voucher. · how: Contrast authorised vaccination redemption with cash withdrawal and other spending. · when: At the point of attempted use. · conditions: Both the service and the authorised centre must match. · atmosphere: A beneficiary presenting the voucher at a vaccination centre._
_skipped steps: 5: The use restriction has no formula._

**b42 · question_to_student · step 1: the fact in one line** — सही सेवा किसी भी centre पर मिल जाए तो चलेगा? नहीं, centre authorised भी होना चाहिए।
  _screen: New focus: authorised provider condition, not merely the right service._

**b43 · example · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — ₹1,000 का coupon और ₹1,000 cash बराबर रकम दिखाते हैं, खर्च की बराबर आजादी नहीं।
  _screen: Equal face value: ₹1,000 voucher versus ₹1,000 cash. Unequal spending freedom._

**b44 · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — ‘Only’ और ‘authorized’ दोनों पढ़िए; cash और दूसरी खरीदारी पर रोक खुले खर्च का रास्ता बंद करती है।
  _screen: Highlight restrictive wording together: only / authorized / not cash / not elsewhere._

**b45 · recap · step 4: what it changes or proves** — इसलिए exam statement में ‘कहीं भी’ जोड़ने से अर्थ बदल जाएगा।
  _screen: Restriction recap: reject 'any centre' or 'any purchase'._

### Part c32.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define MDR through the difference between sale value and merchant receipt. · how: A ₹1,000 card purchase leaves ₹980 after the illustrated ₹20 fee. · when: During card acceptance and the resulting merchant receipt. · conditions: The example assumes 2%; this is not presented as a universal rate. · atmosphere: A kirana seller accepting a customer's card._

**b46 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — किराने का ₹1,000 bill card से दिया, फिर दुकान को कितना मिला? मान लीजिए 2% fee के ₹20 कटे; दुकानदार को ₹980 मिले।
  _screen: Pay ₹1,000; deduct ₹20; merchant gets ₹980._

**b47 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यही Merchant Discount Rate, MDR है: seller हर card transaction पर अपने acquirer bank को fee देता है, यानी merchant की तरफ के bank को।
  _screen: Merchant-side card fee → acquiring bank._

**b48 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — Discount का मतलब buyer को छूट नहीं; buyer ने पूरा ₹1,000 bill दिया है।
  _screen: Not a customer price discount._

**b49 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — इसलिए gross sale और net receipt अलग हैं: यहाँ ₹1,000 की बिक्री, ₹980 की receipt।
  _screen: Gross ≠ net._

### Part c32.2 · structure
_frame: process: Identify the participants sharing the merchant-paid fee. · how: Move from the merchant's acquiring bank to the customer's issuing bank, network and applicable provider. · when: Distinguish fee collection from its distribution. · conditions: Provider or gateway participation is qualified by 'where applicable'; shares are unspecified. · atmosphere: A payment arrangement with several participants rather than one fee recipient._

**b50 · question_to_student · step 1: the whole in context** — पूरी fee एक bank रखता है? नहीं, MDR कई participants में shared होता है।
  _screen: One fee; several recipients._

**b51 · explain · step 2: zoom into each part in a fixed path (outside to inside)** — Merchant का Acquiring Bank card acceptance की व्यवस्था देता है। Customer का Issuing Bank card देता है; यानी एक seller की acceptance से, दूसरा buyer के card से जुड़ा है।
  _screen: Merchant ← acceptance arrangement: Acquiring Bank. Customer ← card provision: Issuing Bank._

**b52 · explain · step 3: function of each part tied to its shape** — Payment messages कैसे पहुँचेंगे? RuPay, Visa या Mastercard जैसा Card Network banks को जोड़ता है। जहाँ लागू हो, Payment Service Provider/Gateway merchant interface से processing तक जानकारी पहुँचाता है, जैसे checkout का दरवाजा।
  _screen: Merchant interface → applicable provider/gateway → processing. Card network connects payment messages between participating banks._

**b53 · explain · step 4: how the parts work together** — Acceptance, card, messages और applicable technical interface मिलकर payment चलाते हैं। इसलिए acquirer को दी fee पूरी उसकी नहीं रहती; MDR participants में share होता है।
  _screen: Acceptance + card provision + message connection + applicable technical interface → shared payment service → shared MDR._

**b54 · recap · step 5: labelled recap** — Acquirer, issuer, network और applicable provider अलग services देते हैं; shared fee बराबर हिस्से नहीं बताती।
  _screen: Labelled participant map; do not assign equal shares._

### Part c32.3 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Identify the direct MDR payer. · how: Locate the deduction on the merchant's receipt rather than adding it to the customer's payment. · when: When separating the buyer's purchase payment from the seller's fee. · conditions: The statement concerns the direct fee obligation. · atmosphere: The customer pays ₹1,000 and the merchant retains ₹980._
_skipped steps: 5: The payer fact contains no separate formula; arithmetic is taught with the figure._

**b55 · question_to_student · step 1: the fact in one line** — Direct MDR payer कौन है? Merchant है, customer नहीं; bill और fee अलग रखिए।
  _screen: MDR payer: merchant._

**b56 · example · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — Buyer ने ₹1,000 दिए, ₹1,020 नहीं; ₹20 seller की receipt से कटे हैं।
  _screen: Customer ₹1,000; merchant ₹980._

**b57 · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — इसका सबूत seller की छोटी receipt है।
  _screen: Highlight merchant receipt: ₹980 after the customer's ₹1,000 payment._

**b58 · recap · step 4: what it changes or proves** — Exam में payer बदलने का trap पकड़िए: purchase customer करता है, MDR merchant देता है।
  _screen: Purchase payer ≠ MDR payer._

### Part c32.4 · figure
_frame: process: Read the card-payment diagram and verify its net receipt. · how: Follow Customer to Merchant to Banks & Payment Network; calculate and subtract the total fee. · when: Read the purchase arrow first, fee-sharing arrow second and net-receipt line last. · conditions: 2% applies to ₹1,000 in this illustration; ₹20 is the total fee, not each participant's share. · atmosphere: Three boxes with two rightward arrows and a net-receipt calculation._

**b59 · question_to_student · step 1: what the figure is and why it is here** — तीन boxes कैसे पढ़ेंगे? Customer से Merchant, फिर Banks & Payment Network; यह payment और fee अलग दिखाता है।
  _screen: Customer → Merchant → Banks & Payment Network._

**b60 · explain · step 2: a reading path across it (labels in order)** — बाएँ buyer है; Pays ₹1,000 arrow seller तक जाती है। यह खरीदारी की पूरी रकम है, fee नहीं।
  _screen: Highlight buyer and purchase-payment arrow._

**b61 · explain · step 2: a reading path across it (labels in order)** — बीच के Merchant में payment आती है; MDR shared arrow दाएँ निकलती है। Seller के पास ₹980 रहते हैं।
  _screen: Incoming purchase payment; outgoing shared fee._

**b62 · explain · step 2: a reading path across it (labels in order)** — दायाँ box कई recipients को समेटता है: दोनों banks, network और applicable provider; एक organisation नहीं।
  _screen: Expand grouped box into previously named participants and network examples._

**b63 · explain · step 2: a reading path across it (labels in order)** — 2% यानी हर ₹100 पर ₹2; ₹1,000 पर ₹20 कटे, इसलिए ₹980 मिले। Percent rate है, रुपये receipt हैं।
  _screen: ₹1,000 × 2 ÷ 100 = ₹20; ₹1,000 − ₹20 = ₹980._

**b64 · explain · step 3: the one thing to remember from it** — ₹20 total fee है, हर recipient का हिस्सा नहीं; 2% इसी example की rate है, universal नहीं।
  _screen: Gross ₹1,000; total fee ₹20; net ₹980; illustrative 2%._

**b65 · recap · step 4: link back to the text it explains** — चित्र definition खोलता है: buyer payment करता है, merchant fee देता है, participants उसे share करते हैं।
  _screen: Purchase → merchant deduction → shared fee._

**b66 · recap** — अंत में तीन पहचान अलग रखिए: RuPay card brand, e-RUPI restricted voucher, MDR merchant की fee। एक सेवा पर रोक समझाता है, दूसरा net receipt।
  _screen: RuPay: brand | e-RUPI: voucher | MDR: fee._

## v14 · Cross-border payments, polymer notes और cryptocurrency · passed after 2 round(s) · continued from an earlier run

Kind: topic video · budget 6.6-8.8 minutes · spoken about 9.0 minutes
Goal: Learner Project Nexus की जरूरत, leadership, participating systems और country–system matching समझेगा; polymer notes के material–durability claims और cryptocurrency के distributed ownership records को fiat तथा CBDC से अलग करेगा।
Hook: अपने देश में payment तुरंत हो जाता है—दूसरे देश के fast-payment system तक पहुँचने में अलग समस्या क्यों आती है?
Story or analogy: पहले national payment systems को जोड़ने की जरूरत से Nexus का mechanism खुलेगा। फिर स्पष्ट topic transition के साथ 3 अलग बदलाव रखेंगे: payment connectivity, note का material और ownership record की व्यवस्था। Polymer तथा crypto को एक जैसी monetary category नहीं बताएँगे।

**b1 · hook** — Domestic payment तुरंत है; विदेश भेजना मुश्किल क्यों?
  _screen: Connectivity → material → ownership: three different changes._

### Part c33.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define Nexus as a BIS Innovation Hub-led connection between national fast payment systems. · how: Follow a hypothetical India–Singapore remittance through separate domestic payment legs. · when: Explain the intended arrangement, not an asserted live corridor. · conditions: Currency conversion and applicable charges remain necessary. · atmosphere: A sender in India wants to support a relative in Singapore._

**b2 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — मानो Singapore में relative को भेजने के लिए पहले ₹0 रखे थे; अब ₹10,000 रखे हैं। UPI Indian हिस्सा संभालेगा।
  _screen: Hypothetical intended design, not live corridor: ₹0 → ₹10,000 principal; UPI._

**b3 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — Connection PayNow तक instruction पहुँचाएगा। Receipt Singapore dollars में होगी; conversion और applicable charges अलग रहेंगे।
  _screen: UPI → intended connection → PayNow; no exchange rate or fees assumed._

**b4 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यह Project Nexus है; Bank for International Settlements, यानी BIS का Innovation Hub इसे lead करता है।
  _screen: Nexus leadership: BIS Innovation Hub._

**b5 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — Multilateral यानी कई देश; cross-border यानी देशों के बीच payments। यह national fast payment systems, यानी FPS, जोड़ता है।
  _screen: Several countries; separate national FPS connected across borders._

**b6 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — National systems अलग रहते हैं; जोड़ना उन्हें 1 domestic system बनाना नहीं है।
  _screen: Separate national FPS remain distinct: connect ≠ replace._

**b7 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — Remittance में Nexus याद करो: अलग national FPS साथ काम करते हैं।
  _screen: Separate FPS working together._

### Part c33.2 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Identify the objective, founding participation and five country–system pairs. · how: Associate each country with its own named national system. · when: After defining the initiative and before explaining connection costs. · conditions: Participation is distinct from leadership; ease is an objective. · atmosphere: A country–system matching exercise anchored in familiar UPI payments._
_skipped steps: 5: These are associations and an objective, with no formula to build._

**b8 · explain · step 1: the fact in one line** — विदेश भेजना दुकान के UPI जैसा आसान हो? यही objective है, identical fees या time की guarantee नहीं।
  _screen: Domestic UPI ease is the objective, not guaranteed performance._

**b9 · explain · step 1: the fact in one line** — India से कौन? UPI founding participant है, यानी शुरुआत के group में है; leader नहीं।
  _screen: India—UPI; one founding participant. Leader: BIS Innovation Hub._

**b10 · explain · step 1: the fact in one line** — Singapore और Malaysia में कौन? Singapore–PayNow और Malaysia–DuitNow हैं; Now समान होने से देश समान नहीं।
  _screen: Country–system matching: Singapore—PayNow; Malaysia—DuitNow. Highlight each complete pair._

**b11 · explain · step 1: the fact in one line** — Philippines और Thailand में? Philippines–InstaPay और Thailand–PromptPay हैं; Pay देखकर interchange मत करना।
  _screen: Country–system matching: Philippines—InstaPay; Thailand—PromptPay. Highlight each complete pair._

**b12 · compare · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — अपने UPI जैसा national system सोचो: यहाँ 5 systems हैं, एक के 5 नाम नहीं।
  _screen: Five distinct national systems._

**b13 · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — Country–system जोड़ी पहचानो; इससे live corridor साबित नहीं होता।
  _screen: Country–system association ≠ operational corridor._

**b15 · recap · step 4: what it changes or proves** — Matching trap याद रखो: Singapore–PayNow, Thailand–PromptPay; UPI participant है, leader नहीं।
  _screen: Five-pair table; emphasize PayNow versus PromptPay._

### Part c33.3 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Explain intermediary costs and the benefit of shared connection standards. · how: Add an illustrative handling stage, then contrast separate interfaces with a shared design. · when: During remittance routing and payment-system integration. · conditions: Fees and delays vary; connection standardization does not abolish currencies or legal requirements. · atmosphere: A bank transfer counter and a payment-integration desk._

**b16 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — खर्च क्यों बढ़े? Hypothetical ₹10,000 principal, अलग ₹100 fee, 1 working day मानो; outlay ₹10,100 है।
  _screen: Teaching assumptions, not bank quotes: ₹10,000 + ₹100; 1 working day._

**b17 · example · step 2: the cause** — बीच का bank ₹50 और 1 working day जोड़ता है। Fee ₹150, outlay ₹10,150, समय 2 working days हुआ।
  _screen: Principal unchanged at ₹10,000; charges paid separately; no conversion assumed._

**b18 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — ये correspondent banks payment आगे पहुँचाते हैं। Direct arrangement न हो, तो बीच की handling खर्च और समय बढ़ा सकती है।
  _screen: Intermediary handling → possible charges and delay._

**b19 · example · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — ₹1000 instruction कैसे भेजें? Illustratively, 4 partner-specific designs की जगह team ने 1 shared design अपनाया; 3 partners हों, तब भी 1 साझा तरीका अपनाएँगे।
  _screen: Hypothetical integration model, not a live corridor: ₹1000 instruction; 4 partner designs → 1 shared design. Three-partner variant: 3 → 1._

**b21 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — यही Nexus का standardized connection है: हर partner का अलग link नहीं बनाना पड़ता; counts illustrative हैं।
  _screen: Shared rules reduce repeated bilateral integration, not actual software counts._

**b22 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — हर transfer की chain समान नहीं; standardization conversion, कानून या responsibilities नहीं मिटाता।
  _screen: Usually ≠ always; cross-border obligations remain._

**b23 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — Handling खर्च बढ़ा सकती है; shared standard connection-work घटाता है।
  _screen: Payment friction → need for shared connectivity._

### Part c34.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define polymer notes by their supporting material. · how: Replace cotton-based paper with security-grade BOPP while retaining face value. · when: A hypothetical authorized note is used in a purchase. · conditions: The example does not establish Indian circulation. · atmosphere: A kirana purchase worth ₹10._

**b24 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — ₹10 note की sheet बदले तो value बदलेगी? Hypothetically 1 authorized BOPP note लो; सामान ₹10 का ही है।
  _screen: Hypothetical purchase: polymer notes 0 → 1; cotton-paper replaced, value ₹10 unchanged. Not rollout evidence._

**b25 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यह polymer note है: substrate यानी आधार-sheet cotton-based paper नहीं, security-grade Biaxially Oriented Polypropylene, यानी BOPP है।
  _screen: Cotton-based paper versus security-grade BOPP substrate._

**b26 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — Biaxially oriented यानी बनाते समय film को लंबाई-चौड़ाई जैसी 2 दिशाओं में खींचते हैं।
  _screen: Two perpendicular stretching arrows._

**b27 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — Ordinary plastic पर ₹10 लिखना authorized banknote बनाना नहीं है।
  _screen: Non-example: marked ordinary plastic._

**b28 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — आगे durability में यही material-change काम आएगा।
  _screen: Material changes, not face value; next: durability._

### Part c34.2 · comparison
_frame: process: Distinguish engineered banknotes from ordinary plastic and coexistence from withdrawal. · how: Compare security design and the treatment of existing paper notes. · when: When interpreting a proposed material change. · conditions: A different substrate alone neither creates a banknote nor cancels existing currency. · atmosphere: A shopkeeper checks notes and keeps valid paper notes in use._

**b29 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Plastic और banknote समान हैं? Material-family मिल सकती है; banknote specially engineered होता है।
  _screen: Common material family; different engineered purpose._

**b30 · explain · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — Embedded security यानी बनावट में पहचान, जैसे ID की निशानी। Paper notes भी साथ चलेंगे, जैसे wallet में पुराने-नए designs।
  _screen: Built-in authenticity identification; paper and polymer streams remain alongside each other._

**b31 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — पहचान imitation पकड़ने में मदद करती है। Coexistence दोनों को चलने देता है; withdrawal एक kind हटाता है।
  _screen: Authenticity checks; coexistence versus withdrawal._

**b32 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — Material change को demonetisation मत समझना: existing paper notes बेकार नहीं बताए गए हैं।
  _screen: Material change ≠ invalidation._

**b33 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Engineered note ordinary plastic नहीं; साथ चलना replacement नहीं।
  _screen: Two-row comparison recap._

### Part c34.3 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Interpret proposed denominations and the lifespan multiplier. · how: Compare a baseline lifespan with an upper-bound multiplier. · when: When assessing the proposed small-denomination introduction. · conditions: Up to 4x is not an exact outcome or four extra years. · atmosphere: Frequently handled ₹10 and ₹20 notes._

**b34 · explain · step 1: the fact in one line** — Small notes में किनसे शुरुआत का proposal है? RBI के ₹10, ₹20; lifespan claim up to 4x है।
  _screen: Proposed ₹10, ₹20; up to 4x lifespan._

**b35 · explain · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — Baseline 1 bar हो, तो upper comparison 4 bars है; 4 extra years नहीं।
  _screen: L versus 4L; no years assigned._

**b36 · misconception · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — Claim का up to upper limit बताता है, हर note का measured result नहीं।
  _screen: Upper limit ≠ exact achieved outcome; claim undated._

**b37 · explain · step 4: what it changes or proves** — छोटे notes बार-बार हाथ बदलते हैं; इसलिए wear और replacement की जरूरत जाँचना उपयोगी होगा।
  _screen: Frequent handling → practical durability assessment._

**b38 · recap · step 5: a formula built term by term when there is one** — Baseline गुणा 4 upper comparison है; denominations ₹10, ₹20 हैं।
  _screen: Upper comparison = baseline × 4; up to, not exact._

### Part c34.4 · timeline
_frame: process: Place international introduction and Indian trial approval in chronological order. · how: Move from Australia in 1988 to Government approval in 2013. · when: Distinguish introduction, approval and wider adoption. · conditions: Approval is not proof of nationwide circulation; adoption figures have no supplied reference date. · atmosphere: International experience alongside a limited Indian testing proposal._

**b39 · explain · step 1: the period and its atmosphere** — नई sheet से पहले किसका अनुभव देखें? Introduction, testing से अलग है।
  _screen: International introduction versus Indian testing._

**b40 · explain · step 2: events in order with their causes** — Australia ने 1988 में पहले introduce किया। India में 2013 की ₹10 field-trial approval सिर्फ limited testing की हाँ थी।
  _screen: 1988 Australia: first introduction; 2013 India: ₹10 trial approval._

**b41 · explain · step 3: the people and their choices** — Government ने approval दी; असली handling में जाँच से laboratory से छूटी परेशानियाँ मिल सकती हैं।
  _screen: Government choice: limited real-use test._

**b42 · misconception · step 4: the turning point** — Event पहचानो: approval से trial completion या nationwide circulation साबित नहीं होती।
  _screen: Approval ≠ completion ≠ national rollout._

**b43 · explain · step 5: what changed after** — 50 से ज्यादा countries का इस्तेमाल experience देता है, Indian savings का प्रमाण नहीं।
  _screen: >50 countries: comparative experience, not proof of Indian cost savings._

**b44 · recap · step 6: timeline recap** — 1988 introduction था; 2013 Indian trial approval थी।
  _screen: 1988 Australia introduction → 2013 Government approval of ₹10 Indian field trial._

### Part c34.5 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Explain durability, anti-counterfeiting and replacement-cost objectives. · how: Reduce illustrative unfit notes, then separate wear resistance from imitation resistance. · when: Over comparable periods of handling and replacement. · conditions: Illustrative quantities are not measured savings; objectives are not achieved outcomes. · atmosphere: A bank cash counter collecting worn notes._

**b45 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — खराब notes कम हों तो क्या बचेगा? मानो 100 ₹10 notes खराब हुए; उतने ही समय और handling में durable material के सिर्फ 50 खराब हुए।
  _screen: Hypothetical equal-period comparison under comparable handling: 100 unfit notes versus 50; not measured results._

**b46 · explain · step 2: the cause** — Longer life से collection और replacement घटें: यही durability से replacement costs घटाने का उद्देश्य है।
  _screen: Durability → fewer replacements → lower recurring work._

**b47 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — दूसरा अलग लक्ष्य counterfeiting कठिन करना है: wear रोकना और imitation रोकना अलग काम हैं।
  _screen: Goals: longer life, harder counterfeiting, lower replacement costs._

**b48 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — Counts illustration हैं, measured savings नहीं।
  _screen: Illustration ≠ measured savings._

**b49 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — बनाना सस्ता न भी हो, कम replacement बचत देगा। Durable होना counterfeit-proof होना नहीं।
  _screen: Production cost ≠ replacement cost; durable ≠ counterfeit-proof._

**b50 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — Durability replacement घटाए; security imitation रोके।
  _screen: Two separate benefit chains; objectives, not guaranteed outcomes._

### Part c35.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define cryptocurrency through a blockchain-recorded digital transfer. · how: Track changes in hypothetical holdings and explain linked records. · when: After a transfer is validated. · conditions: Fees are ignored in the illustration; digital form does not establish official monetary status. · atmosphere: Two users transferring a hypothetical digital asset._

**b51 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — बिना note ownership कैसे बदले? आपकी 2 units थीं, receiver की 0। Fees छोड़कर 1 भेजी; आपको 1 बची, उसे 1 मिली। जाँच के बाद linked blocks में record बना।
  _screen: Hypothetical transfer; fees ignored: authorize 1 → validate → linked-block record; sender retains 1, recipient receives 1._

**b52 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यह cryptocurrency है: blockchain से created और stored digital/virtual currency। Blockchain जुड़े blocks का record है, जैसे जुड़े हिसाब-पन्ने।
  _screen: Blockchain: linked blocks containing transaction records._

**b53 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — हर phone balance crypto नहीं; wallet transfer की keys संभालता है, literal coins नहीं।
  _screen: Wallet manages cryptographic keys; blockchain maintains transaction records._

**b54 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — Blockchain record official money नहीं बनाता।
  _screen: Blockchain technology ≠ official monetary status._

### Part c35.2 · comparison
_frame: process: Separate privately created crypto from RBI-issued digital money. · how: Compare issuer and legal-tender status rather than digital appearance. · when: When classifying an asset as official money or a private asset. · conditions: Absence of legal-tender status alone does not determine legality of holding or trading. · atmosphere: Two digital balances with different institutional status._

**b55 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Crypto और Digital Rupee phone पर समान लगें? Digital रूप समान है, issuer और legal status अलग।
  _screen: Cryptocurrency | Digital Rupee: both digital; compare issuer and legal status next._

**b56 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — Crypto private asset है, RBI-issued नहीं; Digital Rupee RBI-issued CBDC है। India में पहला legal tender नहीं, दूसरा है।
  _screen: Cryptocurrency | Digital Rupee. Issuer: private, not RBI | RBI. Indian legal tender: no | yes._

**b57 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — Legal tender यानी कानून से payment चुकाने के लिए स्वीकार किया गया money। केवल transferable होना काफी नहीं।
  _screen: Legal tender: legally recognized money for discharging payment obligations. Transferability alone is insufficient._

**b58 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — Not legal tender सीधे illegal नहीं; holding, trading और tax अलग सवाल हैं।
  _screen: Legal-tender status, holding/trading rules and taxation are separate questions._

**b59 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Crypto private, legal tender नहीं; Digital Rupee RBI का, legal tender है।
  _screen: Difference table: Cryptocurrency | Digital Rupee. Digital: yes | yes. Issuer: private, not RBI | RBI. Indian legal tender: no | yes._

### Part c35.3 · process
_frame: process: Explain ownership recording through a distributed ledger. · how: Validate a proposed transfer and update records maintained across participating computers. · when: A valid transfer is accepted under network rules. · conditions: An unaccepted instruction does not establish changed ownership; network details vary. · atmosphere: Several computers maintain a consistent ownership record._

**b60 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — Bank की अकेली copy बिना हिसाब कैसे चले? मानो आपकी 3 units थीं, receiver की 0। Fees छोड़कर 1 भेजी; transfer accept हुआ, आपको 2 बचीं, उसे 1 मिली।
  _screen: Hypothetical accepted transfer, fees ignored: sender begins with 3; sends 1; retains 2. Recipient begins with 0 and receives 1._

**b61 · process_walkthrough · step 2: inputs and the place** — आपकी भेजने की instruction network computers तक पहुँची; वे authorization और उपलब्ध units जाँचते हैं।
  _screen: Input: authorized transfer instruction. Participating computers check authorization and available unspent units._

**b62 · explain · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — क्या instruction आपकी है और 3 units में भेजी जाने वाली 1 पहले खर्च नहीं हुई? Shared rules से जाँच सही निकले, तो computers ownership बदलना स्वीकार करते हैं। यही distributed ledger है: कई computers पर मिलता हिसाब, bank या government की अकेली copy नहीं।
  _screen: Check authorization → check sufficient unspent units → accept under shared network rules → update distributed ownership records._

**b63 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — गलत authorization या दोबारा वही unit खर्च करने की कोशिश reject होगी; ownership नहीं बदलेगी। Exact rules networks में अलग होते हैं।
  _screen: Invalid authorization or repeated spending → reject; no accepted ownership change. Exact rules vary by network._

**b64 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — जाँच सही थी; records बदले, आपको 2 बचीं, उसे 1 मिली।
  _screen: Replay: authorize → check unspent units → accept → update holdings._

**b65 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — साझा जाँच से ownership का हिसाब मिलता है।
  _screen: Shared checks → accepted transfer → matching distributed ownership records._

### Part c35.4 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Explain distrust and payment grievances as motivations for alternatives. · how: Trace mortgage default to loss, and a merchant fee to lower net receipts. · when: During financial distress or costly and incompatible payment experiences. · conditions: Motivation proves neither crypto safety nor freedom from fees; crisis losses do not prove dollar purchasing-power erosion. · atmosphere: A hypothetical US mortgage lender and an Indian kirana payment counter._

**b66 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — Banking पर भरोसा क्यों हिले? मानो weak-credit borrower पर $100000 बाकी थे; वह हर महीने $800 देता था, फिर repayment रुक गई।
  _screen: Hypothetical US borrower: $100000 outstanding; previously paid $800 monthly, then paid nothing._

**b67 · example · step 2: the cause** — घर से net $70000 मिले: $30000 shortfall हुआ। कई loans और mortgage-backed claims के losses institutions कमजोर कर सकते हैं।
  _screen: Other recoveries ignored: $100000 − $70000 = $30000 loss._

**b68 · example · step 2: the cause** — Charges से परेशानी क्यों? Hypothetical ₹100 card sale में ₹1 fee कटे; merchant को ₹99 मिले।
  _screen: Illustrative fee, not actual tariff: ₹100 → ₹99 proceeds._

**b69 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — यह subprime distress है: losses से distrust, फिर alternatives में interest हो सकता है। Anarchist groups central authority का विरोध करते हैं।
  _screen: Loan losses → financial disruption → possible distrust → interest in alternatives._

**b70 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — यह merchant acceptance charge MDR, यानी Merchant Discount Rate, समझाता है: fee से दुकानदार की receipt घटी। E-banking/card charges और interoperability की परेशानी भी motives हैं; interoperability यानी अलग systems साथ काम करें।
  _screen: Hypothetical acceptance fee: ₹100 sale − ₹1 fee = ₹99 merchant receipt. MDR: Merchant Discount Rate. Interoperability: systems working together._

**b71 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — कुछ anarchist groups ने USA की 2007 Subprime Crisis से dollar की purchasing power घटने को FIAT MONEY पर distrust का कारण माना; loan losses यह साबित नहीं करते।
  _screen: Attributed claim: crisis → dollar purchasing-power erosion → fiat distrust. Loan losses do not establish that purchasing-power claim._

**b72 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — FIAT MONEY monetary/legal system से चलता है, fixed-gold conversion से नहीं। Purchasing power यानी रकम कितना सामान खरीदे; loan loss इसका घटना साबित नहीं करता।
  _screen: Fiat: monetary/legal backing, not fixed-gold convertibility. Purchasing power: goods and services a sum buys. Loan loss ≠ proof of purchasing-power erosion._

**b73 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — Crypto भी risky है; fees और compatibility limits मिटती नहीं।
  _screen: Seeking an alternative does not establish safety, zero fees or universal compatibility._

**b74 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — Distrust और charges motives हैं, guarantees नहीं।
  _screen: Motivations ≠ guaranteed benefits._

**b75 · recap** — Connectivity, material, private records: तीनों अलग रखो।
  _screen: Nexus: payment connectivity | Polymer: note material | Crypto: private ownership records. Next: money supply._

## v15 · Money supply: M0 से M4 तक क्या गिना जाता है? · passed after 2 round(s) · continued from an earlier run

Kind: topic video · budget 9.0-12.0 minutes · spoken about 12.1 minutes
Goal: Learner aggregate overview से M0–M4 की पूरी family समझेगा; formulas को component by component बनाएगा और currency in circulation, public currency, RBI deposits, demand deposits, time deposits तथा post-office coverage की boundaries पहचान सकेगा।
Hook: जेब का cash, bank balance और fixed deposit—देश का money गिनते समय क्या सबको हर बार एक ही total में जोड़ेंगे?
Story or analogy: पहले अलग जगह रखी रकम की situation, फिर मापने की जरूरत और aggregate names आएँगे। Overview के बाद M0 का base अलग समझाएँगे; M1–M4 को component cards से बनाएँगे। M1–M2 और M3–M4 की direct comparison, liquidity, NSC exclusion और inclusion–exclusion checks होंगे; M0 को बाकी aggregates की सीधी nested सीढ़ी नहीं दिखाएँगे।

**b1 · hook** — Cash, bank balance और FD—क्या हमेशा सब जोड़ेंगे?
  _screen: Wallet, bank balance and FD; question mark over total._

### Part c36.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define the economy's money holdings at an instant. · how: Transfer ₹100 between two holders, then distinguish the unchanged stock from payments. · when: Measure at one selected instant, not across a month. · conditions: Specify eligible monetary components and avoid repeated counting. · atmosphere: A buyer pays a neighbourhood kirana shop._

**b2 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — खरीदार के ₹100, दुकानदार के ₹0 हैं। खरीदारी के बाद दोनों के balances उलट गए, लेकिन combined cash ₹100 ही रहा।
  _screen: Buyer ₹100 → ₹0; shopkeeper ₹0 → ₹100; total unchanged._

**b3 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — Money supply economy में एक तय पल पर spending, saving और payments के लिए उपलब्ध money है। Holdings की photo सोचो; यह stock है।
  _screen: Freeze economy-wide holdings at one instant._

**b4 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — महीने के payments का total यह stock नहीं है। वही ₹100 बार-बार इस्तेमाल हो सकता है।
  _screen: Repeated payments with one ₹100 note versus one snapshot._

**b5 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — यानी तय पल की holdings गिनेंगे। Cash और deposits इस्तेमाल करने की आसानी अलग है, इसलिए अलग measures चाहिए।
  _screen: Snapshot plus component boundary._

**b5a · example** — Bank के RBI account में ₹100 हैं। RBI उससे ₹100 की security खरीदकर account में जोड़ता है; अब ₹200 हैं। यह बढ़ा balance payments का आधार बनेगा।
  _screen: Hypothetical security purchase; reserve balance ₹100 → ₹200. Delay aggregate names._

**b5b · example** — ₹2000 में से ₹1000 का certificate खरीदो। किराने के लिए ₹1000 cash बचा; certificate नहीं दोगे। यह भविष्य की बचत National Savings Certificate, यानी NSC है।
  _screen: Cash ₹2000 → ₹1000; certificate ₹0 → ₹1000. Reveal name after the purchase._

### Part c36.2 · classification
_frame: process: Organize the five aggregate names by coverage. · how: Keep M0 separate; branch M2 and M3 from M1 and extend M3 to M4. · when: Overview precedes detailed formulas. · conditions: Overlapping aggregates must not be added together. · atmosphere: A household and shop hold cash and several kinds of deposits._

**b6 · example · step 1: the overview tree first: the basis of division and every member named** — Hypothetical holdings लो: cash ₹1000, demand account ₹4000, bank time deposit ₹6000, Post Office savings ₹2000 और दूसरे eligible deposits ₹3000 हैं। Other RBI deposits zero मानो।
  _screen: Holdings cards; separate ₹5000 NSC holding outside the eligible baskets. No aggregate labels yet._

**b7 · example · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — Cash और demand account के ₹5000 हुए। Post Office savings जोड़कर ₹7000 मिलेंगे। इसके बजाय ₹5000 में bank time deposit जोड़ो: ₹11000 मिलेंगे; eligible Post Office deposits भी जोड़ो तो ₹16000 होंगे।
  _screen: Four unlabelled component baskets: ₹5000, ₹7000, ₹11000, ₹16000; NSC excluded._

**b8 · example · step 3: the members compared side by side, the most confused pair named directly** — Demand account से ₹1000 तय समय वाली deposit में रखो। अब उनमें ₹3000 और ₹7000 हैं। पहले totals ₹4000 और ₹6000 होंगे; दोनों bank balances वाले totals ₹11000 और ₹16000 ही रहेंगे।
  _screen: Move ₹1000 between deposit columns. Recalculate all four unlabelled totals._

**b9 · recap · step 4: a memory trick for the family** — ये M1, M2, M3 और M4 हैं। M3 Broad Money, M4 broadest है; RBI-created base M0, Reserve Money है। याद रखो: M2 और M3, M1 से निकलने वाली branches हैं; overlapping totals मत जोड़ना।
  _screen: Reveal names; M0 separate, M2 and M3 branch from M1, M4 extends M3. M3 is the principal reporting measure._

### Part c36.3 · figure
_frame: process: Read and qualify the five-tier money pyramid. · how: Scan apex to base, then replace misleading cumulative implications with component baskets. · when: After the family overview and before detailed mechanisms. · conditions: Printed abbreviations are not complete definitions. · atmosphere: Five widening tiers beside an accurate branch diagram._

**b10 · question_to_student · step 1: what the figure is and why it is here** — Pyramid नीचे चौड़ा होता दिख रहा है? इसकी 5 tiers M0 से M4 तक cash और deposits की coverage पहचानने में मदद करती हैं।
  _screen: Five widening tiers, apex to base; retain all component labels and no arrows._

**b10a · explain · step 2: a reading path across it (labels in order)** — ऊपर से पढ़ो: M0, फिर M1 में Cash + Demand Deposits। अगले खाने में M2 के साथ PO savings हैं; PO यानी Post Office, जहाँ savings passbook रखते हो।
  _screen: Reading path: apex M0 → M1 Cash + Demand Deposits → M2 + PO Savings Deposits. Show PO passbook image._

**b10b · explain · step 2: a reading path across it (labels in order)** — फिर M3 पर Bank Time Deposits, जैसे FD दिखती है। सबसे नीचे M4 पर All PO Deposits except NSC है; savings certificate की पर्ची बाहर रखो।
  _screen: Continue reading path through M3 and M4. FD receipt beside bank time deposits; NSC outside the qualifying PO deposit basket._

**b14 · misconception · step 3: the one thing to remember from it** — अलग टोकरियाँ सोचो: M0 अलग रखो। M3 के लिए M1 वाली टोकरी में bank time deposits डालो, M2 वाली में नहीं। पूरी M1 टोकरी में OD भी आता है।
  _screen: Replace the cumulative ladder with baskets. Show M1 + bank time deposits = M3; include OD within M1._

**b15 · recap · step 4: link back to the text it explains** — याद रखो: tiers labels पहचानने के लिए हैं; जोड़ने के लिए सही component baskets इस्तेमाल करो।
  _screen: Pyramid beside corrected component baskets; M0 separate, M2 and M3 branching from M1._

### Part c36.4 · process
_frame: process: Money moves through payments and deposits. · how: Follow a household payment to a shop and its bank, with a separate bank–RBI link. · when: During purchases, wages, deposits and lending. · conditions: A payment generally transfers money; every household payment need not directly involve RBI. · atmosphere: A local shop connected to the banking system._

**b16 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — किराने पर दिए ₹500 कहाँ गए? पहले दुकानदार को इस sale से ₹0 मिले थे; अब ₹500 मिले, जो उसने bank में जमा किए।
  _screen: ₹500 household payment followed by shop deposit._

**b17 · explain · step 2: inputs and the place** — यह money circulation है, यानी दुकान पर दिया पैसा आगे चलता है। Households, businesses और banks जुड़े हैं; banks का RBI से connection है।
  _screen: Follow the shop payment into its bank; show a separate bank–RBI connection._

**b18 · process_walkthrough · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — Purchases, wages, deposits और loans पर पैसे चलते हैं। RBI transactions banks को जोड़ते हैं; हर खरीदारी सीधे RBI से नहीं गुजरती।
  _screen: Animate payment paths and separate RBI transactions._

**b19 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Recipient अगला payment रोक दे तो आगे movement रुकेगा। पैसा गायब नहीं हुआ; अभी उसके पास है।
  _screen: Pause next payment; balance stays with recipient._

**b20 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — फिर देखो: खरीदार ने दिया, दुकानदार ने bank में जमा किया।
  _screen: Replay purchase and bank deposit._

**b21 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — Circulation movement है; Money supply तय पल की holdings है। पैसा चलना हमेशा नया पैसा बनना नहीं है।
  _screen: Flow versus stock._

### Part c37.1 · definition
_frame: process: Identify directly RBI-created reserve money. · how: An RBI security purchase credits a bank's reserve account. · when: Central-bank money provides a base for subsequent banking activity. · conditions: Reserves support settlement and potential lending, not compulsory lending. · atmosphere: RBI and a commercial bank's reserve account._

**b22 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — Bank का RBI balance ₹200 कैसे हुआ? पहले ₹100 थे; security खरीदकर RBI ने ₹100 जोड़े थे।
  _screen: Replay the ₹100 purchase and resulting ₹200 reserve balance._

**b23 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — सीधे RBI-created base M0 है: Reserve Money या High-Powered Money। Foundation की तरह यह आगे lending से bank money बनने का आधार है।
  _screen: M0 foundation supports additional bank money._

**b24 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — तुम्हारा deposit और bank का RBI balance अलग claims हैं। RBI balance withdrawals और banks के बीच settlement सँभालने में मदद करता है।
  _screen: Customer → commercial bank claim; commercial bank → RBI claim. Attribution: Bank of England, Money creation in the modern economy, Quarterly Bulletin, 2014 Q1._

**b25 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — M0 आधार है। उससे आगे bank lending की गुंजाइश बनती है, lending अपने-आप नहीं होती।
  _screen: Base supports, but does not compel, lending. Attribution: Bank of England, Money creation in the modern economy, 2014 Q1._

### Part c37.2 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Construct M0 from three monetary stocks. · how: Distinguish circulating currency, other RBI deposits and bankers' RBI deposits. · when: Use balances for the same instant. · conditions: Use common units; include bank vault cash and CRR balances without double counting. · atmosphere: Public wallets, a bank vault and accounts held at RBI._

**b26 · example · step 1: the fact in one line** — Public cash ₹1000, vault cash ₹200 और bank का RBI balance ₹800 हो तो कितना base पैसा है? Other RBI deposits zero मानकर total ₹2000 है।
  _screen: Hypothetical monetary-base holdings: ₹1000 + ₹200 + ₹800 = ₹2000; no formula labels yet._

**b27 · example · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — Bank RBI account से ₹100 के notes लेता है। Vault cash ₹300 और RBI balance ₹700 हो जाता है। M0 ₹2000 रहता है; public cash नहीं बढ़ता।
  _screen: Currency in circulation ₹1200 → ₹1300; bankers' RBI deposits ₹800 → ₹700; M0 unchanged at ₹2000._

**b28 · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — C में public और bank cash हैं। Bankers' Deposits में CRR balances भी हैं; Cash Reserve Ratio यानी relevant liabilities का तय हिस्सा bank RBI में रखता है।
  _screen: C = Currency in circulation: public currency + bank cash. Bankers' Deposits include CRR balances._

**b29 · explain · step 4: what it changes or proves** — Account किसका, कहाँ? Bank का RBI account Bankers' Deposits है; eligible दूसरे RBI deposits OD हैं। तुम्हारा commercial-bank account OD नहीं: वह RBI में नहीं है।
  _screen: Holder–institution–category table. Do not classify every non-bank RBI account automatically as OD._

**b30 · recap · step 5: a formula built term by term when there is one** — M0 = C + OD + Bankers' Deposits। एक ही date और unit के balances जोड़ो; customer के commercial-bank deposits इसमें मत डालना।
  _screen: Three component boxes; same date and monetary unit. Attribution: RBI, Handbook of Statistics on the Indian Economy, 2023–24._

### Part c37.3 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Bank lending creates deposit money across the banking system. · how: An ₹80 loan and a subsequent ₹60 loan create ₹140 of deposits under stated assumptions. · when: Lending and interbank settlement occur in successive rounds. · conditions: Reserves, capital, liquidity, lending standards and credit demand constrain expansion. · atmosphere: Two banks, a business borrower and a supplier._

**b31 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — Loan से balance कैसे बढ़ता है? सारी ज़रूरी शर्तें पूरी मानो: Bank A ₹80 loan deposit बनाता है; borrower Bank B के supplier को देता है।
  _screen: ₹80 loan creates deposit; supplier receives payment at Bank B._

**b32 · example · step 2: the cause** — Bank B शर्तें पूरी करके ₹60 का दूसरा loan देता है। पहले इन loans के deposits नहीं थे; repayments और cash leakage छोड़ें तो अब ₹140 बने हैं।
  _screen: Two illustrated loans create ₹80 + ₹60 = ₹140 of deposits._

**b33 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — यह reserves-supported deposit expansion है। Loan deposit बनाता है; दूसरे bank को payment जाने पर settlement के लिए reserves चाहिए।
  _screen: Separate deposit creation from reserve settlement. Attribution: Bank of England, Money creation in the modern economy, 2014 Q1._

**b34 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — Supplier रकम account में रखता है तो deposit banking system में रहता है।
  _screen: Follow the illustrative supplier receipt into its bank account. Attribution: Bank of England, Money creation in the modern economy, 2014 Q1._

**b35 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — Expansion guaranteed नहीं है। Withdrawals, liquidity और capital needs, lending standards तथा loan demand इसे सीमित करते हैं; reserves मिलना unlimited lending की छूट नहीं है।
  _screen: Constraints gate further lending. Attribution: Bank of England, Money creation in the modern economy, 2014 Q1._

**b36 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — Loan deposit बनाता है; payment settle होता है। आगे lending शर्तों पर निर्भर है, इसलिए expansion system-wide और conditional है।
  _screen: Deposit creation → settlement → conditional further lending. Attribution: Bank of England, Money creation in the modern economy, 2014 Q1._

### Part c38.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define M1 through near-immediate spendability. · how: Demonstrate a cash purchase and a demand-account payment before explaining Narrow Money. · when: Funds are needed for everyday transactions. · conditions: Almost immediate use does not mean every form of savings qualifies. · atmosphere: A customer paying for groceries._

**b37 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — ₹200 का किराना अभी चाहिए? ₹500 cash से दोगे तो ₹300 बचेंगे; ₹1000 payment-ready account से ₹300 दो तो ₹700 बचेंगे।
  _screen: Two separate immediate-payment examples._

**b38 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यह immediate-payment focus M1, Narrow Money की पहचान है। यह public का सबसे liquid money है, यानी जल्दी इस्तेमाल होने वाला पैसा।
  _screen: M1: most liquid; almost immediately spendable._

**b39 · compare · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — FD में access की शर्तें हो सकती हैं। Narrow तुरंत इस्तेमाल वाले components हैं, पतले notes नहीं।
  _screen: FD outside immediate-payment boundary._

**b40 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — M1 almost immediately spendable components गिनता है। अब formula बनाओ।
  _screen: Payment-ready components become formula cards._

### Part c38.2 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Build M1 and M2 component by component. · how: Add public currency, demand deposits and other RBI deposits; then add eligible post-office savings deposits. · when: Compare updated balances after completed transfers. · conditions: Exclude bank vault cash from public currency and NSC from the M2 addition. · atmosphere: Wallet, bank account and post-office passbook._

**b41 · example · step 1: the fact in one line** — Cash ₹1000, demand balance ₹4000 और other RBI deposits zero हों तो ₹5000 हुए। Post Office savings ₹2000 भी गिनें तो ₹7000 होंगे; किस boundary में क्या आएगा?
  _screen: Hypothetical contribution, not national totals. Calculate ₹5000 and ₹7000 before revealing aggregate labels._

**b42 · example · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — Wallet के ₹500 Post Office में जमा करो। Cash ₹500, savings ₹2500 हैं। M1 में ₹4500 बचे; passbook जोड़ो तो ₹7000। यह M2, Intermediate Money है, यानी wallet से passbook में गया पैसा भी गिनेंगे।
  _screen: After settlement, move ₹500 from wallet to PO savings passbook. Show ₹4500 + ₹2500 = ₹7000, then reveal M2. Keep both holdings inside the M2 boundary._

**b43 · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — Demand deposits में on-demand current और savings balances आते हैं। M1 में bank vault cash और Bankers' Deposits नहीं; OD दोनों formulas में है।
  _screen: M0 versus M1 inclusion table._

**b44 · example · step 4: what it changes or proves** — Original balances पर लौटो। Bank से ₹500 निकालो तो cash ₹1500, demand ₹3500: M1 ₹5000 रहा। Account की पुरानी रकम फिर जोड़ोगे तो double counting होगी।
  _screen: Reset original cash ₹1000 and demand ₹4000; withdraw ₹500; sum updated balances only._

**b45 · recap · step 5: a formula built term by term when there is one** — M1 में public currency, Demand Deposits और OD जोड़ो। M2 में इसके ऊपर Post Office savings deposits जोड़ो; bank time deposits और NSC नहीं।
  _screen: Build M1 and M2 formulas; excluded cards outside. Attribution: RBI, Handbook of Statistics on the Indian Economy, 2023–24._

### Part c38.3 · classification
_frame: process: Classify post-office products as included or excluded in the M2 addition. · how: Separate savings-bank deposits from NSC and similar instruments. · when: Before adding post-office holdings to M1. · conditions: Product type, not the institution alone, determines inclusion. · atmosphere: Two different savings products offered at a post office._

**b46 · question_to_student · step 1: the overview tree first: the basis of division and every member named** — Post Office passbook और certificate दोनों जोड़ेंगे? M2 addition में savings-bank deposits included हैं; NSC और similar instruments excluded हैं।
  _screen: Two-branch product classification._

**b47 · explain · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — Savings deposit खाते की रकम है; NSC लंबे समय का certificate है। जगह एक, लेकिन product और access अलग हैं; गिनती भी अलग होगी।
  _screen: Parallel product cards: account deposit versus long-term certificate; inclusion versus exclusion._

**b48 · compare · step 3: the members compared side by side, the most confused pair named directly** — Deposit और certificate अलग हैं; Post Office नाम देखकर दोनों मत जोड़ना।
  _screen: Deposit included; certificate excluded._

**b49 · recap · step 4: a memory trick for the family** — Exam trick: M2 में eligible savings deposit है, हर savings product नहीं।
  _screen: Read product type, not merely institution._

### Part c38.4 · comparison
_frame: process: Compare liquidity and convenience of savings deposits. · how: Contrast availability of funds with ease of accessing or using them. · when: A saver needs money for a payment. · conditions: Relative inconvenience does not imply inaccessibility; no current restrictions are assumed. · atmosphere: A saver comparing bank and post-office account access._

**b50 · question_to_student · step 1: what is common** — दो accounts में ₹1000 हों तो ₹300 का bill देना बराबर आसान होगा? दोनों में रकम है; अब इस्तेमाल का रास्ता देखो।
  _screen: Hypothetical equal balances: ordinary bank deposit ₹1000 and PO savings ₹1000; grocery bill ₹300._

**b51 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — मानो bank account से सीधे bill दो, पर चुने हुए Post Office access में पहले cash निकालो। रकम दोनों में है; दूसरे रास्ते में extra step है।
  _screen: Hypothetical paths only: direct account payment versus withdrawal then payment. Not a universal restriction._

**b52 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — यह hypothetical है, universal restriction नहीं। रकम निकाल सकना liquidity है; payment के steps convenience बदलते हैं।
  _screen: Hypothetical access paths; availability versus access steps._

**b53 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — Fairly liquid और slightly less convenient साथ हो सकते हैं। Less convenient का मतलब inaccessible नहीं।
  _screen: Less convenient ≠ inaccessible._

**b54 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Post Office savings fairly liquid हैं, ordinary bank deposits से थोड़ी कम convenient; availability और access अलग हैं।
  _screen: Comparison table: liquidity and relative convenience._

### Part c39.1 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Construct M3 and M4 and distinguish importance from breadth. · how: Add bank time deposits to M1, then qualifying post-office deposits to M3. · when: Use same-date stocks; reset assumptions for separate worked examples. · conditions: M3 does not start from M2; M4 excludes NSC and counts post-office savings once. · atmosphere: A bank FD conversion, a monetary-statistics desk and a news headline._

**b55 · example · step 1: the fact in one line** — Bank में ₹1000 तय समय के लिए रखो तो तुरंत खर्च करोगे? जल्दी निकालने की शर्तें हो सकती हैं। ये Time Deposits हैं; FD, recurring deposits और दूसरे bank time deposits M3 में आते हैं।
  _screen: Agreed-term ₹1000 deposit; identify FD, RD and other bank time deposits._

**b57 · example · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — M1 ₹5000 में bank time deposits ₹6000 जोड़ो: ₹11000, यानी M3 या Broad Money। फिर Post Office savings ₹2000 और दूसरे eligible deposits ₹3000 जोड़ो: ₹16000, यानी M4; NSC बाहर है।
  _screen: Hypothetical contributions, OD zero. Calculate before revealing M3 and M4 labels; NSC card excluded. PO savings counted once._

**b58 · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — News में money-supply growth आम तौर पर M3 है। M4 broadest है, यानी M3 की टोकरी में eligible Post Office deposits भी डालो, NSC नहीं। Recurring deposits ऐसे समझो: हर महीने बचत की किस्त जमा करना।
  _screen: M3: Broad Money, most important and usual news reference. M4: broadest. Place eligible PO deposits into the M3 basket; leave NSC outside. Show monthly RD instalments._

**b58a · explain · step 4: what it changes or proves** — इसलिए सबसे बड़ा घेरा हमेशा सबसे important indicator नहीं है। M4 की coverage चौड़ी है; फिर भी news में M3 देखोगे। दोनों भी सारी wealth नहीं गिनते: NSC बाहर है।
  _screen: Contrast coverage with reporting importance: M4 broadest; M3 principal news measure; excluded NSC remains outside monetary coverage._

**b62 · recap · step 5: a formula built term by term when there is one** — M3 में M1 और bank time deposits जोड़ो, M2 नहीं। M4 में M3 और eligible Post Office deposits जोड़ो; NSC बाहर है, savings को दोबारा मत जोड़ना।
  _screen: Component formulas; count qualifying PO savings once. Attribution: RBI, Handbook of Statistics on the Indian Economy, 2023–24._

### Part c39.2 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Explain RBI monitoring and the NSC exclusion. · how: Trace loan-created deposits into monetary indicators, then contrast spendable cash with a long-term certificate. · when: Policy analysis compares monetary growth with credit, output and prices. · conditions: M3 is an indicator, not proof of inflation; specific exclusions do not exclude all term deposits. · atmosphere: A local business loan and a family's long-term savings decision._

**b63 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — नया loan क्या बदलता है? पहले M3 में ₹1000 थे; bank ने ₹100 loan दिया, supplier ने रकम account में रखी। बाकी बदलाव न हों तो अब ₹1100 गिनेंगे।
  _screen: New lending increases illustrative M3 by ₹100._

**b65 · explain · step 2: the cause** — Loan से बढ़े balances पर RBI नज़र रखता है। Certificate अलग है: वह भविष्य की बचत का डिब्बा समझो, आज किराने पर देने वाला wallet नहीं। इसलिए NSC, M4 से बाहर है।
  _screen: Connect loan-created balances to monitoring; contrast future savings certificate with today's grocery wallet._

**b66 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — M3 liquidity और credit conditions के संकेत देता है। अगर spending उपलब्ध सामान से तेज़ बढ़े तो दामों पर दबाव बन सकता है; इसलिए money growth को output और prices के साथ पढ़ेंगे।
  _screen: Conditional links; compare monetary holdings, credit, output and prices. Attribution: RBI, Report on Currency and Finance, 2021–22._

**b67 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — Policy effectiveness जाँचने के लिए देखेंगे: RBI के कदमों के बाद money, credit और prices चाही दिशा में गए या नहीं। M3 अकेला फैसला नहीं देता।
  _screen: Assess money, credit and prices together after policy action._

**b68 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — इसलिए M3 के साथ credit और price evidence देखो; Post Office product होने भर से NSC को शामिल मत करो।
  _screen: Evidence dashboard and explicit NSC exclusion. Attribution: RBI, Report on Currency and Finance, 2021–22._

**b69 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — M3 बढ़ना inflation का proof नहीं; यह loans का total भी नहीं। NSC बाहर है, लेकिन bank time deposits M3 में आते हैं।
  _screen: Reject three confusions. Attribution: RBI, Report on Currency and Finance, 2021–22; Handbook of Statistics on the Indian Economy, 2023–24._

**b70 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — RBI के checks हैं inflationary pressures, liquidity, credit growth और policy effectiveness। NSC long-term, readily spendable नहीं; इसलिए M4 से बाहर है।
  _screen: Four monitoring gauges; NSC exclusion chain._

**b71 · recap** — आखिरी check: किसका balance, कहाँ, कौन-सा product? इसी से सही aggregate चुनोगे।
  _screen: Final holder–institution–product checklist beside the aggregate map: M0 separate; M2 and M3 branch from M1._

## v16 · PYQ अभ्यास: blockchain, RTGS–NEFT और BHIM · passed after 4 round(s) · reused

Kind: topic video · budget 4.8-6.5 minutes · spoken about 6.6 minutes
Goal: Learner supplied questions के हर statement और option का कारण सहित निर्णय करेगा; blockchain applications और permission, settlement–charges–hours तथा BHIM interoperability और authentication की traps अलग पहचान सकेगा।
Hook: किसी statement का पहला हिस्सा सही हो, लेकिन आखिरी शब्द गलत हो—क्या आप उसे फिर भी सही चुनेंगे?
Story or analogy: हर supplied PYQ में पहले learner का निर्णय, फिर statement को छोटे claims में तोड़कर mechanism से जाँच होगी। Blockchain का ledger structure और applications जोड़ेंगे; technology को cryptocurrency तक सीमित नहीं करेंगे। Pre'25 wording में charge direction और hours, तथा BHIM में authentication factors और transaction steps अलग जाँचेंगे।

**b1 · hook** — आधा statement सही हो, तो पूरा चुनोगे?
  _screen: आधा सही ≠ पूरा सही | 2020 • 2025 • 2018_

### Part c40.2 · process
_frame: process: Accept an authorised delivery record without limiting the technology to cryptocurrency. · how: Submit, validate, agree and replicate; reject unsupported overwrites. · when: The entry becomes accepted only after required checks and agreement. · conditions: Application authorisation and reliable reporting remain necessary; open development does not waive law. · atmosphere: A hypothetical public-network onion delivery application._

**b2 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — Seller ने 10 प्याज के sacks पहुँचाए; साझा हिसाब कैसे बनेगा? पहले कोई entry नहीं थी; अब उसने 10 sacks की entry भेजी।
  _screen: Hypothetical mandi: seller delivers 10 sacks and submits an entry._

**b3 · example · step 2: inputs and the place** — Seller ने authorised record भेजा; application के rules के मुताबिक buyer की confirmation भी जोड़ी। Participating computers ने इसे जाँचा।
  _screen: Authorised delivery report → participating validators_

**b4 · example · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — Authorisation और format सही मिले; network के agreement rules पूरे हुए। तभी 10 sacks की entry साझा accepted history में जुड़ी।
  _screen: Check authorisation and format → agreement → accept 10 sacks_

**b5 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Viewer ने 10 मिटाकर 20 लिखा, लेकिन unsupported बदलाव accepted नहीं हुआ। यह blockchain delivery application है; validation असल delivery का proof नहीं, उसके लिए भरोसेमंद reporting चाहिए।
  _screen: Local overwrite: 10 → 20 rejected | Blockchain application: delivery records_

**b6 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — Report से checks, फिर agreement से entry बनी। Permissionless features पर development को central owner की मंजूरी नहीं चाहिए; कानून और private-network access rules फिर भी लागू हैं।
  _screen: Report → checks → agreement → entry | Open development ≠ unrestricted access or conduct_

**b7 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — Checks से जुड़ा delivery record cryptocurrency का record नहीं है।
  _screen: Delivery report → validation → shared record | Not cryptocurrency-only_

### Part c40.1 · structure
_frame: process: Separate public inspection from unilateral control of a shared ledger. · how: Move from the shared record to network copies and validation rules. · when: Reading is possible independently of proposing and accepting updates. · conditions: The question assumes a public blockchain; restricted networks need qualification. · atmosphere: A hypothetical mandi with buyer and seller inspecting matching delivery records._

**b8 · explain · step 1: the whole in context** — अब यह साझा हिसाब कहाँ रहता है? Public blockchain का ledger यानी electronic हिसाब, जैसे कई लोगों के पास matching account books।
  _screen: Public blockchain ledger: shared electronic record_

**b9 · explain · step 2: zoom into each part in a fixed path (outside to inside)** — Entry की copies participating computers पर रहती हैं; accepted history network के validation और agreement rules से बनती है।
  _screen: Entry → network copies → validation and agreement_

**b10 · explain · step 3: function of each part tied to its shape** — Shared rules अकेले user को accepted history बदलने नहीं देते। Public inspection यानी देख सकना, editing का अधिकार नहीं है।
  _screen: Inspection ≠ unilateral editing authority_

**b11 · explain · step 4: how the parts work together** — Proposal पर checks होते हैं; agreement से shared history बदलती है। अपनी copy बदलने से बाकी network नहीं बदलता।
  _screen: Proposal → checks → agreement → accepted history_

**b12 · recap · step 5: labelled recap** — Ledger साझा है; control अकेले का नहीं है।
  _screen: Ledger | distributed copies | shared acceptance rules_

### Part c40.q · none
_frame: process: Evaluate three blockchain statements and four combinations. · how: Separate inspection, subject matter and development permission. · when: After the ledger and delivery mechanisms are understood. · conditions: Use the public, permissionless context intended by the question. · atmosphere: UPSC Prelims 2020 statement-combination practice._

**b13 · exam_tip** — 2020 में public permissionless context में 1 और 3 सही; 2 का ‘cryptocurrency only’ गलत। तुम्हारा combination क्या होगा?
  _screen: 1 ✓ 2 ✗ 3 ✓ | Public, permissionless context_

**b13a · exam_tip** — a गलत है क्योंकि सही statement 3 छूटता है। b भी गलत है क्योंकि गलत 2 जुड़ता है और सही 3 छूटता है।
  _screen: a: omits 3 ✗ | b: includes 2, omits 3 ✗_

**b13b · exam_tip** — c सिर्फ गलत 2 चुनता है। d सही: 1 और 3, यानी shared control और open development।
  _screen: c: 2 only ✗ | d: 1 and 3 only ✓_

### Part c41.2 · process
_frame: process: Trace one hypothetical stock payment under two alternative settlement methods. · how: Authorisation and bank checks precede individual gross or batch net settlement and credit. · when: Individual real-time settlement versus half-hourly batch cycles. · conditions: Valid instructions, system checks and available funds are required. · atmosphere: A shop owner paying a supplier at another bank._

**b14 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — ₹3,00,000 का stock खरीदा, तो क्या हुआ? Buyer के ₹5,00,000 में ₹2,00,000 बचे। Supplier के पास ₹1,00,000 थे; payment मिलने पर ₹4,00,000 हुए।
  _screen: Hypothetical completed purchase: ₹3,00,000 | Buyer ₹5,00,000 → ₹2,00,000 | Supplier ₹1,00,000 → ₹4,00,000_

**b15 · example · step 2: inputs and the place** — Buyer ने bank A से payment authorise किया; bank ने जाँचा। Supplier bank B में था; उसी दौर में दूसरे customer ने B से A को ₹2,00,000 भेजे।
  _screen: A → B: ₹3,00,000 | B → A: ₹2,00,000 | Compare alternative settlement methods_

**b16 · process_walkthrough · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — पहले तरीके में पूरी रकम अलग-अलग settle हुई। दूसरे में batch का net ₹1,00,000 A से B गया; customers को पूरी रकम मिली। पहला RTGS, Real Time Gross Settlement; दूसरा NEFT, National Electronic Funds Transfer है। RTGS का minimum ₹2,00,000 है।
  _screen: RTGS: ₹3,00,000 and ₹2,00,000 separately | Minimum ₹2,00,000 | NEFT batch: ₹3,00,000 − ₹2,00,000 = ₹1,00,000 A → B | Full customer amounts_

**b17 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Checks या funds अधूरे हों, तो settlement अटक सकता है। App notification का मतलब बैंकों का हिसाब पूरा होना नहीं है।
  _screen: Checks + funds required | Notification ≠ interbank settlement_

**b18 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — Supplier payment में authorisation, checks, bank settlement और beneficiary credit आते हैं; notification अलग पहचानो।
  _screen: Authorise → check → settle → credit | Notification is distinct_

**b19 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — Gross में पूरा payment अलग; net में आपसी बाकी रकम settle होती है।
  _screen: Gross: full individual obligations | Net: offset bank obligations_

### Part c41.1 · comparison
_frame: process: Compare bank-transfer systems on three independent criteria. · how: Align settlement mode, inward charges and operating availability. · when: Evaluate availability in the question's 2025 setting. · conditions: Inward refers to the recipient; availability is not settlement speed. · atmosphere: A supplier checking receipt of a customer's bank payment._

**b20 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Supplier को दोनों तरीकों से पैसा मिलता है; फर्क कहाँ है? Settlement यानी भुगतान के पीछे बैंकों का आपसी हिसाब पूरा होना।
  _screen: Compare settlement, inward charges and operating hours_

**b21 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — RTGS हर payment real time में अलग settle करता है; NEFT half-hourly batches में। Inward यानी खाते में पैसा आना: दोनों में receiving customer के लिए free है; दोनों 24×7 हैं।
  _screen: RTGS: individual real time | NEFT: half-hourly batches | Both: inward free, 24×7_

**b22 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — NEFT में batch का इंतजार है; RTGS में नहीं। RBI ने NEFT को 16 December 2019 और RTGS को 14 December 2020 से round-the-clock किया, दोनों 2025 से पहले।
  _screen: NEFT: 16 December 2019 | RTGS: 14 December 2020 → Both precede 2025_

**b23 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — भेजने का charge, पाने का charge नहीं है। 24×7 खुलना भी real-time settlement नहीं है।
  _screen: Sending ≠ receiving | Availability ≠ settlement mode_

**b24 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Settlement अलग है; दोनों inward-free और 24×7 हैं।
  _screen: Settlement differs | Inward receipts free in both | Both 24×7_

### Part c41.q · none
_frame: process: Select the correct RTGS–NEFT statement combination. · how: Keep the settlement claim and reject false inward-charge and restricted-hours contrasts. · when: In the supplied 2025 examination context. · conditions: Instantaneous describes the settlement model, not guaranteed zero interface delay. · atmosphere: Prelims statement elimination._

**b25 · exam_tip** — 2025 में I सही; instantaneous real-time model है, zero-delay guarantee नहीं। II का inward charge और III के restricted days गलत हैं।
  _screen: I ✓ II ✗ III ✗_

**b25a · exam_tip** — a सही है: I real-time और batch का फर्क बताता है। b गलत है क्योंकि II पाने वाले पर charge लगाता है।
  _screen: (a) I only ✓ | (b) I and II ✗_

**b25b · exam_tip** — c और d में गलत III है: दोनों 24×7 हैं। d सही I भी छोड़ता है।
  _screen: (c) I and III ✗ | (d) III only ✗_

### Part c42.1 · process
_frame: process: Transfer existing money across UPI-enabled bank accounts using BHIM. · how: Identify the payee, verify the amount, authorise and confirm successful processing. · when: Treat payment as completed after successful status and receipt confirmation. · conditions: Eligible linked accounts, registered-device linkage and PIN authorisation are required. · atmosphere: A kirana purchase where shopper and merchant use different apps._

**b26 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — अलग app वाले merchant को ₹500 कैसे मिले? तुम्हारे ₹2,000 में किराने के ₹500 देने पर ₹1,500 बचे। Merchant के ₹3,000 थे; payment मिलने पर ₹3,500 हुए।
  _screen: Hypothetical completed grocery payment: ₹500 | Shopper ₹2,000 → ₹1,500 | Merchant ₹3,000 → ₹3,500_

**b27 · example · step 2: inputs and the place** — तुमने merchant का linked account चुना, नाम और ₹500 जाँचे। Registered device पर UPI PIN डालकर payment authorise किया।
  _screen: Verify payee and ₹500 → authorise on registered device with UPI PIN_

**b28 · process_walkthrough · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — Network और banks ने processing पूरी की, तभी रकम पहुँची। यह BHIM से UPI transfer है; Unified Payments Interface eligible bank accounts जोड़ता है, same app जरूरी नहीं।
  _screen: BHIM → UPI → linked accounts | Existing ₹500 transferred, not created_

**b29 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Amount भरना काफी नहीं; authorisation या processing अधूरी हो, तो payment completed मत मानो।
  _screen: Amount entered ≠ successful payment_

**b30 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — खरीद में payee जाँचो, authorise करो, फिर successful status और receipt confirm करो।
  _screen: Verify → authorise → process → confirm_

**b31 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — BHIM दरवाजा है, UPI रास्ता; पैसा जुड़े accounts में जाता है।
  _screen: App ≠ network ≠ account_

### Part c42.2 · comparison
_frame: process: Compare authentication evidence in chip-and-PIN and BHIM payments. · how: Distinguish possession and knowledge from transaction screens. · when: During authorisation of the respective purchase. · conditions: Use the ordinary card-plus-PIN and registered-device-plus-UPI-PIN models in the question. · atmosphere: A shopper comparing card and mobile payment._

**b32 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Card लगाना और PIN डालना क्या अलग सबूत हैं? Authentication factors यानी पहचान के अलग तरह के सबूत; card और BHIM दोनों में possession और knowledge हैं।
  _screen: Authentication evidence: possession + knowledge_

**b33 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — Card में possession का सबूत card है; BHIM में registered mobile/device linkage। Knowledge दोनों में secret PIN की है।
  _screen: Card + card PIN | Registered device linkage + UPI PIN_

**b34 · analogy · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — Bank को proof चाहिए, clicks नहीं। चाबी और secret code अलग proof हैं; बार-बार चाबी घुमाना नए factors नहीं बनाता।
  _screen: Possession + knowledge | Repeated actions ≠ new factors_

**b35 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — Payee, amount और confirmation screens actions हैं, independent factors नहीं। Chip-pin card को four-factor कहना गलत है।
  _screen: Transaction steps ≠ authentication factors | Four-factor premise ✗_

**b36 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Possession का सबूत अलग है; PIN knowledge दोनों में है।
  _screen: Card versus registered device linkage | PIN knowledge shared_

### Part c42.q · none
_frame: process: Evaluate the BHIM reach and authentication statements. · how: Accept interoperability and reject the four-factor card premise. · when: After the payment sequence and evidence comparison. · conditions: A correct second half cannot rescue a false first half. · atmosphere: UPSC Prelims 2018 option elimination._

**b37 · exam_tip** — 2018 में 1 सही, 2 गलत। BHIM की two-factor बात card का four-factor दावा सही नहीं बनाती; screens गिनना factors गिनना नहीं है।
  _screen: 1 ✓ 2 ✗ | Evaluate both halves_

**b37a · exam_tip** — a सही: UPI-enabled accounts तक पहुँच है। b गलत: false 2 चुनता और सही 1 छोड़ता है।
  _screen: (a) 1 only ✓ | (b) 2 only ✗_

**b37b · exam_tip** — c गलत 2 जोड़ता है। d भी गलत: interoperability वाला सही 1 हटा देता है।
  _screen: (c) Both ✗ | (d) Neither ✗_

**b38 · recap** — Access, hours और screens से control, settlement और factors मत तय करो।
  _screen: Access ≠ control | Hours ≠ settlement | Steps ≠ factors_

## v17 · NPCI, RuPay और MDR: PYQ में कौन किसे पैसे देता है? · passed after 2 round(s) · continued from an earlier run

Kind: topic video · budget 6.5-8.6 minutes · spoken about 8.2 minutes
Goal: Learner दोनों pages में फैले NPCI question को पूरा हल करेगा; inclusion और RuPay संबंधों का कारण समझेगा तथा MDR को merchant charge, incentive, cashback और government support से अलग कर सकेगा।
Hook: Digital payment पर cashback मिले और merchant से fee कटे—क्या दोनों को MDR कह सकते हैं?
Story or analogy: Continued NPCI question को एक पूरे question की तरह रखेंगे; हर claim और option की role–function जाँच होगी। MDR में पहले कौन किसे रकम देता है यह तय करेंगे, फिर definition और सभी distractors का कारण खोलेंगे। अंत में money, legal status, payment route और money supply के फर्क का एक closing recap होगा।

**b1 · hook** — Card से खरीदारी पर आपको cashback मिले और दुकानदार के पैसे से fee कटे—क्या दोनों एक चीज़ हैं? आज 2 PYQ में payment व्यवस्था का काम और पैसे की दिशा जाँचेंगे।
  _screen: Buyer receives cashback | Merchant bears fee; NPCI PYQ → MDR PYQ._

### Part c43.1 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Payment infrastructure can turn account ownership into usable financial access. · how: A hypothetical cross-bank household transfer makes funds available for household needs. · when: Access and linked accounts come first, followed by authorisation, successful transfer and use. · conditions: Participating accounts, connectivity, affordability and user capability matter; infrastructure alone cannot guarantee inclusion. · atmosphere: A worker in a town sends household money to a family member in a village._

**b2 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — शहर में काम करने वाला व्यक्ति गाँव में घर खर्च भेजना चाहता है; सिर्फ दोनों के पास passbook होने से काम पूरा होगा? मान लीजिए उसके खाते में ₹3,000 हैं, परिवार के खाते में ₹200। जरूरत यह है कि ₹1,000 पहुँचें और परिवार उनसे किराना खरीद सके।
  _screen: Hypothetical: town worker ₹3,000; village family ₹200; household transfer needed ₹1,000._

**b3 · explain · step 2: the cause** — रुकावट कहाँ आ सकती है? अगर पैसे भेजने और पाने के लिए एक ही bank होना जरूरी हो, तो अलग banks वाले परिवार को परेशानी होगी। दोनों तरफ जरूरी access और linked accounts हों, और banks साझा payment व्यवस्था में जुड़े हों, तो यह same-bank जरूरत हट सकती है।
  _screen: Different participating banks connected by one payment route; access and linked accounts marked as assumptions._

**b4 · process_walkthrough · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — अब व्यक्ति अपने ₹3,000 में से ₹1,000 भेजता है, तो उसके पास ₹2,000 बचते हैं। परिवार के खाते में पहले ₹200 थे; ₹1,000 मिलने पर अब ₹1,200 हैं। परिवार उपलब्ध payment या withdrawal service से पैसे इस्तेमाल करता है; खाता घर की जरूरत पूरी करने का रास्ता बना।
  _screen: ₹1,000 transfer: ₹3,000 → ₹2,000; ₹200 → ₹1,200; received funds → household use._

**b5 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — काम की financial सेवाएँ लोगों तक पहुँचना और उनका इस्तेमाल कर पाना, यही financial inclusion है। NPCI, यानी National Payments Corporation of India, देश की retail payment व्यवस्था से जुड़ी organisation है। साझा payment रास्ते accounts को काम का बनाते हैं, इसलिए NPCI financial inclusion बढ़ाने में मदद करता है।
  _screen: Financial inclusion: usable access to financial services. NPCI = National Payments Corporation of India. Infrastructure → practical account use._
